Gastronomi ve Mutfak Sanatları Öğretim Üyesi Prof. Dr. Gül Yılmaz’ın Kitabı Yayımlandı
Gastronomi ve Mutfak Sanatları Bölümü öğretim üyelerimizden Prof. Dr. Gül Yılmaz’ın kaleme aldığı "Gastronomide Servis Tekniklerinin Önemi" adlı kitap, Detay Yayıncılık etiketiyle okurlarla buluştu. Mutfak sanatlarının yalnızca yemek hazırlama ve pişirme süreçlerinden ibaret olmadığını, yemeğin misafire ulaştığı anın da gastronomi deneyiminin ayrılmaz bir parçası olduğunu vurgulayan eser, alandaki önemli bir ihtiyaca yanıt veriyor. Prof. Dr. Gül Yılmaz, kitabında servis tekniklerinin gastronomi dünyasındaki yerini, profesyonel sunumun inceliklerini ve kusursuz bir misafir deneyimi yaratmanın temel ilkelerini ele alıyor. Hem Gastronomi ve Mutfak Sanatları öğrencileri hem de yiyecek-içecek sektöründe görev yapan profesyoneller için kapsamlı bir başvuru kaynağı niteliği taşıyan bu çalışma, teorik bilgiyi sektörel uygulamalarla bir araya getiriyor.
Akademisyenimizi tebrik eder, başarılarının devamını dileriz.

İSTANBUL TOPKAPI ÜNİVERSİTESİ

                               KALİTE GÜVENCE YÖNERGESİ



                                     BİRİNCİ BÖLÜM
                    Amaç, Kapsam, Dayanak, Tanımlar ve Temel İlkeler


Amaç
Madde 1 - Bu Yönerge; İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi’nde akademik ve idari hizmetlerin
“Yükseköğretim Kalite Güvencesi Yönetmeliği” kapsamında Yükseköğretim Kalite Kurulu
tarafından belirlenen usul ve esaslar doğrultusunda; iç ve dış değerlendirme raporlarının
hazırlanması, kalite güvence sisteminin kurulması, eğitim ve öğretim, araştırma faaliyetleri ve
idari hizmetlerin iç ve dış kalite güvencesine göre yürütülmesini sağlamak amacıyla kurulan
Üniversite Kalite Komisyonunun, Birim Kalite Komisyonlarının ve Üniversite Kalite
Koordinatörlüğünün oluşumu ve çalışmalarına ilişkin usul ve esasları düzenler.
Kapsam
Madde 2 - Bu usul ve esaslar; İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Kalite Komisyonunun görev ve
faaliyet alanlarını kapsar.
Dayanak
Madde 3 - Bu Yönerge; 4/11/1981 tarihli ve 2547 sayılı Yükseköğretim Kanununun 44’üncü
maddesinin (b) bendine; 23/7/2015 tarihli ve 29423 sayılı Resmî Gazete’de yayımlanarak
yürürlüğe giren “Yükseköğretim Kalite Güvencesi Yönetmeliği”ne dayanılarak hazırlanmıştır.
Tanımlar
Madde 4 - Bu Yönergede geçen;
a) Üniversite: İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesini,
b) Rektör: İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Rektörünü,
c) Senato: İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Senatosunu,
ç) Genel Sekreter: İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Genel Sekreterini,
d) Yükseköğretim Kalite Güvencesi Komisyonu: 23.07.2015 tarih ve 29423 sayılı Resmi
Gazetede yayımlanan Yükseköğretim Kalite Güvencesi Yönetmeliği esaslarına göre kurulmuş
ve Üniversitelerde kalite güvencesi çalışmaları ile akreditasyon çalışmalarının düzenlenmesi
ve yürütülmesinden sorumlu Yükseköğretim Kalite Komisyonunu,
e) Kalite Güvencesi: Üniversitenin veya programının iç ve dış kalite standartları ile uyumlu
kalite ve performans süreçlerini tam olarak yerine getirdiğine dair güvence sağlayabilmek için
yapılan tüm planlı ve sistemli işlemleri,
f) Yönetmelik: 23.11.2018 tarih ve 30604 sayılı Resmi Gazete’de yayımlanan Yükseköğretim
Başkanlığı Kalite Güvencesi Yönetmeliği’ni,
g) Başkan: Kalite Komisyonu Başkanını,
h) Üye: Senato tarafından belirlenmiş Kalite Komisyonu üyelerini,
ı) Birim: İstanbul Ayvansaray Üniversitesine bağlı Fakülte, Enstitü, Yüksekokul, Meslek
Yüksekokulu, Uygulama ve Araştırma Merkezleri, akademik ve idari birimleri,
i) Kalite Güvencesi Alt Komisyonu: Gerekli olduğu durumlarda kurumun kalite iyileştirme ve
geliştirme süreçlerinin etkin işleyişini ve sürekliliğini sağlamak üzere komisyonla koordineli
çalışmak üzere Kalite Komisyonu tarafından oluşturulmuş alt komisyonları,
j) Birim Kalite Komisyonu: Enstitü, Fakülte, Yüksekokul, Meslek Yüksekokulu, Araştırma ve
Uygulama Merkezleri, Genel Sekreterlik ve Müdürlükleri temsil eden komisyonu,
k) Akreditasyon: Dış değerlendirici kurum tarafından belirli bir alanda önceden belirlenmiş
akademik ve alana özgü standartların; İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesinin herhangi bir programı
tarafından karşılanıp karşılanmadığını ölçen değerlendirme ve dış kalite güvence sürecini,
l) Dış Değerlendirme: İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi ya da İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi
programlarının; eğitim ve öğretim, araştırma faaliyetleri ile idarî hizmetlerinin kalitesinin,
Yükseköğretim Kalite Kurulu tarafından yetkilendirilen dış değerlendiriciler ya da
Yükseköğretim Kurulunca tanınan bağımsız Kalite Değerlendirme Tescil Belgesine sahip dış
değerlendirme kuruluşlarınca yürütülen dış değerlendirme sürecini,
m) İç Değerlendirme: İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesinin, eğitim ve öğretim, araştırma
faaliyetlerinin, idarî hizmetlerinin kalitesinin ve kurumsal kalite geliştirme çalışmalarının,
İstanbul      Topkapı   Üniversitesi   Kalite   Komisyonu   tarafından   ya   da   Komisyonun
görevlendireceği değerlendiriciler tarafından değerlendirilmesini,
n) Paydaş: İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Enstitü, Fakülte, Yüksekokul, Meslek Yüksekokulu
ve idari birimlerin hizmetleri ile doğrudan ve dolaylı ilişkili olan veya bu hizmetleri olumlu ya
da olumsuz yönde etkileyen kişi, grup, kurum ve kuruluşları,
o) Türkiye Yükseköğretim Yeterlilikler Çerçevesi (TYYÇ): Yükseköğretim için tanımlanan
Ulusal Yeterlilikler Çerçevesini
ifade eder.
Kalite Güvence Temel İlkeleri

Madde 5 - İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi, Kalite Güvencesi kapsamındaki faaliyetlerini;

a) Kalite anlayışının Üniversitenin tüm çalışanları tarafından benimsenmesi,

b) Üniversitenin tüm birimlerindeki çalışanlarına, kurum aidiyeti ve iş tatmini kazandırabilecek
koşulların oluşturulması,

c) Kalite Yönetimi uygulamalarında üniversitenin öngörülen kalite hedeflerine ulaşma
düzeyinin düzenli olarak ölçülmesinin ve sürdürülebilirliğinin sağlanması,

ç) Çalışanların performans değerlendirmesinin kalite prensipleriyle desteklenmesi,

d) Kalite yönetim süreçlerinin uygulanmasında; katılımcı, Üniversite kaynaklarını daha etkin
ve verimli kullanan, süreçleri iyileştirmeye ve kaliteli hizmet sunmaya dayalı bir kurum
kültürünün oluşturulması,

e) Üniversitenin tüm idari ve akademik süreçlerinde performans standartlarının tespit
edilmesiyle ölçümlerin yapılması, iyileştirme çabalarının kurumsallaştırılması ve ilgililerin bu
süreçlerde sorumluluk alması

ilkelerine göre yürütür.

                                       İKİNCİ BÖLÜM
           Kalite Komisyonunun Oluşumu, Görevleri, Çalışma Usul ve Esasları



Kalite Komisyonun Oluşumu

Madde 6 - (1) Kalite Komisyonu; aynı akademik birimden birden fazla olmamak üzere öğretim
elemanları arasından Senato tarafından seçilen Başkan dahil en az 6 üyeden oluşur. Komisyon
üyeleri arasında Genel Sekreter, Öğrenci Temsilcisi, Strateji Geliştirme ve Planlama Daire
Başkanı ile Mali İşler Daire Başkanı da bulunur. Kalite Komisyonunun başkanlığını Rektör
veya görevlendireceği Kaliteden Sorumlu Rektör Yardımcısı yapar.

(2) Kalite Komisyonu üyelerinin görev süresi üç yıl, Öğrenci Temsilcisinin görev süresi ise bir
yıldır. Görev süresi sona ermeden ayrılan üyenin yerine ya da herhangi bir nedenle boşalan
üyelik için kalan süreyi tamamlamak üzere aynı usul ve esasla yeni bir üye seçilir. Görev süresi
sona eren üye, aynı usul ve esasla yeniden seçilebilir.
(3) Kalite Komisyonu öğrenci temsilcisi, öğrenci konseyi başkanıdır. Öğrenci temsilcisinin
yapacağı görevler ilgili mevzuatlara uygun olarak Komisyon tarafından belirlenir.

(4) Kalite komisyonu, gerekli olduğu durumlarda kurumun kalite iyileştirme ve geliştirme
süreçlerinin etkin işleyişini ve sürekliliğini sağlamak üzere komisyonla koordineli çalışan kalite
güvencesi alt komisyonlarını atayabilir. Bu alt komisyonlar; Eğitim Öğretim Alt Komisyonu,
Araştırma ve Geliştirme Alt Komisyonu, Yönetsel Süreçleri İzleme ve Değerlendirme Alt
Komisyonundan oluşur. Kalite süreçleri ve çerçeve yönetmeliklere göre ek alt komisyonlar
kurulabilir. Adı geçen alt komisyonlar aynı fakülte, enstitü, yüksekokul, meslek yüksekokulu
ve birden fazla olmamak ve farklı bilim alanlarından olmak üzere seçilen en az üç üyeden oluşur
ve üç yıllık süre için görevlendirilir.

Kalite Komisyonunun Görevleri

Madde 7 - (1) Kalite Komisyonunun görevleri aşağıda belirtildiği gibidir:

a) Üniversitenin planı ve hedefleri doğrultusunda, eğitim ve öğretim, araştırma faaliyetleri ile
idari hizmetlerinin değerlendirilmesi ve kalitesinin geliştirilmesiyle ilgili kurumun iç ve dış
kalite güvence sistemini kurmak,

b) Üniversitenin akademik ve idari birimlerinin kalite yönetim sistemi faaliyetlerini Kalite
Koordinatörlüğü aracılığıyla desteklemek,

c) Programların akreditasyon süreçlerine girmesini teşvik etmek ve bu faaliyetlere katkıda
bulunmak,

ç) Üniversitenin planında belirlenmiş olan hedefler çerçevesinde performans göstergelerini
izlemek, yeni hedefler belirlemek ve bu kapsamda yapılacak çalışmaların Yükseköğretim
Kalite Kurulu tarafından belirlenen usul ve esaslar doğrultusunda yürütülmesini sağlamak ve
koordine etmek,

d) Stratejik planda öngörülen kalite hedeflerine ulaşma düzeyini düzenli olarak ölçmek ve
sürdürülebilirliğini sağlamak,

e) Kalite anlayışının tüm çalışanlar tarafından benimsenmesini sağlamak,

f) Kalite yönetim süreçlerinin uygulanmasında; katılımcılığa, Üniversite kaynaklarını daha
etkin ve verimli kullanmaya, süreçleri iyileştirmeye ve kaliteli hizmet sunmaya dayanan kurum
kültürünün oluşturulmasına katkı sağlamak,
g) İç değerlendirme faaliyetlerini yürütmek, kurumsal değerlendirme ve kalite geliştirme
faaliyetlerinin sonuçlarını içeren yıllık “Kurumsal İç Değerlendirme Raporu’”nu hazırlayarak
Senatoya sunmak,

h) Senato tarafından onaylanan yıllık Kurumsal İç Değerlendirme Raporunun Üniversitenin
Web sitesinde yayınlanması ve Yükseköğretim Kuruluna bildirilmesi için Rektörlüğe sunmak,

ı) Dış değerlendirme sürecinin gerektirdiği hazırlıkları yapmak, Yükseköğretim Kalite
Kurulu’na ya da dış değerlendirici kurumlara gerekli desteği vermek,

i) Dış değerlendiriciler tarafından yerinde ziyaretler sonucu hazırlanan ve Yükseköğretim
Kalite Kurulu tarafından kabul edilen “Kurumsal Dış Değerlendirme Raporu”nun Üniversitenin
web sitesinde yayınlanmasını sağlamak,

j) Eğitim, topluma hizmet, araştırma, paydaş ilişkileri, uluslararasılaşma ve diğer ihtiyaç
duyulan konularda üniversite genelinde veya birimler özelinde faaliyet ve hizmet kalitesini
yönetmek, üniversitedeki akademik ve idari birimlerin Stratejik Planda yer alan ölçülebilir
nitelikteki hedefleri ve bu hedeflerle ilgili performans göstergelerini periyodik olarak izlemek
ve değerlendirmek ve her aşamada PUKÖ çevriminin tamamlanmasını sağlamak,

k) Çalışmalarında ve raporlamalarında Üniversite genelinde standardı sağlamak için form,
cetvel, tablo vb. geliştirmek,

l) İç ve dış değerlendirme raporları sonucuna göre ortaya çıkacak ve iyileştirmeye gereksinim
duyulan alanlarla ilgili gerekli çalışmaları yapmaktır.

Kalite Komisyonunun Çalışma Usul ve Esasları

Madde 8 - (1) Komisyon, aşağıda belirtilen usul ve esaslara göre çalışır:

a) Kalite Komisyonu her akademik yılda en az 3 kez, bunların dışında komisyon başkanının
daveti ya da komisyon üyelerinin salt çoğunluğunun yazılı başvurusuyla toplanır.

b) Toplantı gündemi, tarihi ve yeri Komisyon Başkanı tarafından belirlenir ve bu bilgiler
“Kalite Koordinatörlüğü” tarafından üyelere duyurulur.

c) Komisyon, üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğu ile toplanır ve toplantıya katılanların salt
çoğunluğu ile karar alır. Oyların eşit olması durumunda Başkanın oyu doğrultusunda karar
verilmiş sayılır.

ç) Komisyonun ofis ve personel destek hizmetleri, Kalite Koordinatörlüğü tarafından sağlanır.
                                        ÜÇÜNCÜ BÖLÜM

                     Kalite Koordinatörlüğünün Oluşumu ve Görevleri



Kalite Koordinatörlüğünün Oluşumu

Madde 9 - (1) Kalite Koordinatörlüğü, Rektörlüğe bağlı olarak; Kalite Koordinatörü ve Kalite
Koordinatör Yardımcılarından oluşur.

(2) Komisyon üyeleri arasından Rektör tarafından üç yıllığına Kalite Koordinatörü
görevlendirilir. Koordinatörün önerisiyle Üniversite personeli arasından en fazla dört yardımcı
Rektör tarafından görevlendirilir. Görev süresi biten Koordinatör ve Koordinatör Yardımcıları
aynı usul ile tekrar görevlendirilebilir.

(3) Koordinatör Yardımcıları, Koordinatörün verdiği görevleri yerine getirir ve Koordinatörün
görevi başında bulunmadığı zamanlarda Koordinatöre vekalet eder. Koordinatörün görevinin
sona ermesiyle birlikte Koordinatör Yardımcılarının da görev süreleri biter.

(4) Koordinatör veya Koordinatör Yardımcılarının 6 aydan fazla süreyle görevleri başında
bulunmamaları durumunda görevleri kendiliğinden sona erer.

Kalite Koordinatörlüğünün Görevleri

Madde 10 - (1) Koordinatörlüğün görevleri;

a) Bu Yönergeyle belirlenen usul ve esaslar ile Kalite Komisyonunun kararları doğrultusunda
gerekli faaliyetlerin yürütülmesinde,

b) Birim Kalite Komisyonunun oluşturulmasında, çalışma esaslarının belirlenmesinde ve
faaliyetlerinin izlenmesinde,

c) Üniversite ve akademik birimlerin kalite hedeflerinde yer alan ölçülebilir nitelikteki
hedeflerinin ve bu hedeflerle ilgili performans göstergelerinin süreli olarak izlenmesinde,

ç) Üniversite ve akademik birimler tarafından yeni hazırlanacak kalite hedefleri için öngörülen
hedeflerin ve performans göstergelerinin belirlenmesinde,

d) Üniversitenin kalite hedefleri doğrultusunda eğitim ve öğretim, araştırma faaliyetlerinde,
idari hizmetlerin değerlendirilmesinde ve kalitenin geliştirilmesinde,

e) Üniversitenin iç ve dış kalite güvence sisteminin kurulması faaliyetlerinde,
f) Kalite Komisyonuna ya da Birim Kalite Komisyonlarına destek vermektir.



                                   DÖRDÜNCÜ BÖLÜM
        Birim Kalite Komisyonunun Oluşumu, Görevleri, Çalışma Usul ve Esasları



Birim Kalite Komisyonunun Oluşumu

Madde 11 - (1) Her Fakülte/Enstitü/Yüksekokul/Meslek Yüksekokulunun ve her idari birimin
Birim Kalite Komisyonu bulunmalıdır.

(2) Birim Kalite Komisyonu; ilgili birimlerde Dekan/Müdür/Başkan, Dekan/Müdür/Başkanın
hazır bulunmadığı hallerde yardımcısının başkanlığında; akademik birimlerde tercihen kalite
konusunda tecrübeli öğretim elemanları arasından Dekan/Müdür/Başkan tarafından atanan bir
kişi ve birim sekreteri dahil olmak üzere en az üç kişiden oluşur. Öğrencisi olan birimlerde
birimin öğrenci temsilcisi de komisyonda yer alır. İdari birimlerdeki Kalite Komisyonu ise
birim başkanı ile birim başkanının atayacağı iki idari personelden oluşur. İlgili idari birimin
personel sayısının yeterli olmaması halinde Rektör tarafından diğer idari birimlerdeki
personellerden görevlendirme yapılabilir.

(3) Birim Kalite Komisyonu üyelerinin görev süresi 3 yıldır. Görev süresi sona ermeden ayrılan
üyenin yerine veya herhangi bir nedenle boşalan üyelik için kalan süreyi tamamlamak üzere
aynı usul ve esasla yeni bir üye seçilir. Görev süresi sona eren üye, aynı usul ve esasla yeniden
seçilebilir.

Birim Kalite Komisyonunun Görevleri

Madde 12 - (1) Birim Kalite Komisyonunun görevleri:

a) İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Kalite Komisyonunun belirleyeceği usul ve esaslar
doğrultusunda faaliyetleri yürütmek,

b) Birimin kalite hedeflerini hazırlamak,

c) Üniversitenin hedefleri doğrultusunda, birimdeki akademik ve idari hizmetlerin
değerlendirilmesi, kalitenin geliştirilmesi ve akreditasyon sürecinde gerçekleştirilecek
faaliyetlerin yürütülmesini sağlamak,

ç) “Birim İç Değerlendirme Raporu”nu hazırlamak,
d) Birimde Kalite Yönetim Sistemi ile ilgili bağımsız değerlendirme kuruluşları tarafından
yapılacak belgelendirme ve sonrası değerlendirme çalışmaları için gerekli hazırlıkları yapmak,
bu kuruluşlara her türlü desteği sağlamak,

e) Kalite Komisyonunun kararlarını ilgili birimin alt birimlerine duyurmak, uygulamalarına
katkı sağlamak ve sonuçlarını izlemek,

f) Birimin faaliyetleriyle ilgili olarak hazırlayacakları yıllık raporu Kasım ayı sonuna kadar
Kalite Komisyonuna sunmaktır.

Birim Kalite Komisyonunun Çalışma Usul ve Esasları

Madde 13 - (1) Birim Kalite Komisyonu aşağıda belirtilen usul ve esaslar doğrultusunda çalışır:

a) Birim Kalite Komisyonu her akademik yılda en az 3 kez ve bunların dışında Birim Kalite
Komisyonu Başkanının çağrısı ya da kurul üyelerinin salt çoğunluğunun yazılı başvurusuyla
toplanır.

b) Toplantı gündemi, tarihi ve yeri Birim Kalite Komisyonu Başkanı tarafından belirlenir ve
üyelere duyurulur.

c) Birim Kalite Komisyonu üye tam sayısının salt çoğunluğuyla toplanır ve toplantıya
katılanların salt çoğunluğuyla karar alır. Oylamada eşitlik çıkması durumunda Başkanın
bulunduğu taraf çoğunluk sayılır.



                                      BEŞİNCİ BÖLÜM
                                    Değerlendirme Süreçleri



İç Değerlendirme Süreci ve Takvimi

Madde 14 - (1) Üniversite, eğitim-öğretim ve araştırma faaliyetlerini ve bunları destekleyen
idari hizmetlerin tümünü içine alacak şekilde stratejik plan ve yıllık olarak, performans
programı ve faaliyet raporu ile bütünleşik yapıda bir iç değerlendirme raporu hazırlar.
Üniversitede yapılacak iç değerlendirmeler;

a) Yükseköğretimin ulusal strateji ve hedefleri ışığında belirlenmiş misyonu, vizyonu ve
stratejik hedefleri ile kalite güvencesine yönelik olarak belirlenen politika ve süreçlerini,
b) Akademik birimlerin ölçülebilir nitelikteki hedeflerini, bu hedeflerle ilgili performans
göstergelerini ve bunların periyodik olarak gözden geçirilmesini,

c) Programların TYYÇ ile ilişkili ve öğrenme çıktılarına dayalı olarak yapılandırılması ve
akreditasyon sürecinin gereklerinin yerine getirilmesi konusundaki çalışmalarını,

ç) Bir önceki iç ve dış değerlendirmede ortaya çıkan ve iyileştirilmeye ihtiyaç duyulan alanlarla
ilgili çalışmalarını içerir.

(2) Birim Kalite Komisyonları ilgili dönem içinde yaptığı çalışmalarını Kasım ayının son
gününe kadar Kalite Komisyonuna sunar. Sürecin takibi Kalite Koordinatörlüğünce
gerçekleştirilir.

(3) Kalite Komisyonu, Aralık ayı içinde birimin iç değerlendirme raporunu inceleyerek birime
geri bildirim olarak Birim Geri Bildirim Raporu düzenler.

(4) Kalite Koordinatörlüğü, iç değerlendirme çalışmalarını her yıl ocak ayı sonuna kadar
tamamlar ve Kalite Komisyonu’na gönderir. Kalite Komisyonu, Kalite Koordinatörlüğü’nün iç
değerlendirme çalışmalarını değerlendirir ve İç Değerlendirme Raporu’nu Şubat ayında
tamamlar. Üniversite, hazırlanan İç Değerlendirme Raporu’nu YÖKAK tarafından bildirilen
tarih aralığında Yükseköğretim Kalite Kurulu’na gönderir.

Dış Değerlendirme Süreci ve Takvimi

Madde 15 - (1) Üniversite, belirli periyotlarda, Yükseköğretim Kalite Kurulu tarafından
yürütülecek düzenli bir kurumsal dış değerlendirme süreci kapsamında değerlendirilmekle
yükümlüdür. Üniversitenin dış değerlendirme takvimi, Yükseköğretim Kalite Kurulu
tarafından hazırlanır ve ilan edilir.

(2)   Üniversitenin     dış    değerlendirilmesi,   Yükseköğretim   Kalite   Kurulu    tarafından
yetkilendirilen dış değerlendiriciler veya Yükseköğretim Kurulu tarafından Kalite
Değerlendirme Tescil Belgesi verilmiş bağımsız kurum ve kuruluşlarca gerçekleştirilir.

(3) Birim/program düzeyinde akreditasyona yönelik dış değerlendirme hizmeti, Kalite
Değerlendirme Tescil Belgesine sahip ulusal veya uluslararası bağımsız bir kurumca
gerçekleştirilir ve birim/program ile sınırlıdır.

(4) Dış değerlendirmenin birim/program düzeyinde yapılması durumunda değerlendirme
konuları, değerlendirilecek olan birim/programın faaliyet/hizmet alanı konuları ile sınırlıdır.
İç ve Dış Değerlendirme Sonuçlarının Kamuoyuna Açıklanması

Madde 16 - (1) Üniversitede yapılan iç ve dış değerlendirmelerin sonuçları kamuoyuna açıktır.
Üniversitenin yıllık iç ve dış değerlendirme raporları üniversite internet ana sayfasında
yayımlanır.

                                       ALTINCI BÖLÜM
                                     Çeşitli ve Son Hükümler


Yönergede Hüküm Bulunmayan Haller

Madde 17 - Bu Yönergede hüküm bulunmayan hâllerde 23/7/2015 tarihli ve 29423 sayılı
Resmî Gazete’de yayımlanarak yürürlüğe giren “Yükseköğretim Kalite Güvencesi
Yönetmeliği” hükümleri uygulanır.

Yürürlük

Madde 19 - Bu Yönerge, İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Senatosu tarafından kabul edildiği
tarihten itibaren yürürlüğe girer.

Yürütme

Madde 20 - Bu Yönerge hükümlerini, İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi Rektörü yürütür.

                           teknolojinin kütüphaneye girmesi için çalışmalar yapar.
                           Kütüphane koleksiyonunun oluşmasını, geliştirilmesini, güncel tutulması
                           ve korunmasını sağlar.

2026–2030 STRATEJİK PLANI

                                       HEDEF KARTLARI
         Stratejik amaç ve hedefler, performans göstergeleri, başlangıç değerleri ve 2026–2030 hedef değerleri




                                                         Ocak 2026




İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi           ELEKTRONİK NÜSHA · BASILI HALİ KONTROLSÜZ KOPYA                     Sayfa 1 / 14
                                                                        İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi 2026–2030 Stratejik Planı · Hedef Kartları



Hedef Kartları Hakkında
Bu belge, İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi 2026–2030 Stratejik Planında yer alan stratejik amaç ve hedeflerin hedef kartlarını
içermektedir. Her hedef kartında hedefin bağlı olduğu stratejik amaç, hedefe ilişkin performans göstergeleri, göstergelerin
hedefe etkileri, plan dönemi başlangıç değerleri ve 2026–2030 dönemi yıllık hedef değerleri yer almaktadır.

    • Hedefe etkisi: Göstergenin hedefin gerçekleşmesine katkı ağırlığıdır. Ağırlıklar Stratejik Plan hedef kartı şablonunda
      belirlenmiş olup her hedefte göstergelerin toplamı %100’dür.
    • Başlangıç değeri: 2025 yılı gerçekleşmesidir. Değerler birimlerin hedef kartlarından derlenmiş olup 2020–2025 Stratejik
      Planı Dönem Sonu Değerlendirme ve Kapanış Raporundaki değerlerle aynıdır.
    • Hedef değerleri: 2026–2030 Stratejik Planında yer alan değerlerdir.
    • Kurum değerleri: Verinin üretildiği birimden alınarak belirlenmiştir. Sayı ve tutar göstergelerinde birim değerleri
      toplanmış, oran göstergelerinde ölçüm yapan birimlerin ortalaması alınmıştır. Kütüphane, sürekli eğitim, lisansüstü
      program ve kurum geneli anket göstergelerinde ilgili birimin kayıtları esas alınmıştır.
    • İzleme ve raporlama: Tüm göstergeler yıllık olarak izlenmekte ve raporlanmaktadır.
    • Başlangıç değeri bulunmayan göstergeler “—” ile gösterilmiştir.




İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi             ELEKTRONİK NÜSHA · BASILI HALİ KONTROLSÜZ KOPYA                                           Sayfa 2 / 14
                                                                                            İstanbul Topkapı Üniversitesi 2026–2030 Stratejik Planı · Hedef Kartları


Stratejik Amaçlar ve Hedefler
 Amaç 1              Eğitim-Öğretimde Niteliği Arttırmak
                     Öğrencilerin sektöre yönelik bilgilerini ve mesleki becerilerini arttıracak eğitim, etkinlik, seminer düzenlemek, bu konular hakkında bilgi
 Hedef 1.1
                     vererek öğrencileri yönlendirmek (3 gösterge)
 Hedef 1.2           Kariyer günleri kapsamında meslek öncüleriyle öğrencileri buluşturacak konferans, seminer gibi etkinlikler düzenlemek (1 gösterge)
                     Program müfredatını ve ders içeriklerini güncel tutmak, seçmeli dersleri çeşitlendirmek, ders tasarımında teknoloji çağının
 Hedef 1.3
                     gerekliliklerini ve iç-dış paydaş geri dönüşlerini dikkate almak (4 gösterge)
                     Bölümün ulusal ve uluslararası düzeyde akreditasyonuna yönelik olarak gerekli başvuru süreçlerini başlatmak, devam ettirmek veya
 Hedef 1.4
                     sonuçlandırmak (3 gösterge)
 Hedef 1.5           Mesleki gelişimi artırıcı kurs ve sertifika programları düzenlemek (5 gösterge)
                     Eğiticinin eğitimi konusunda programlar düzenlenmesini ve/veya akademik personelin düzenlenen eğitimlere katılımını sağlamak (6
 Hedef 1.6
                     gösterge)
 Hedef 1.7           Mevcut lisansüstü programlarının sayı ve niteliğini arttırmak (3 gösterge)
 Amaç 2              Kurumda Kalite Kültürü Oluşturmak
 Hedef 2.1           İç ve dış paydaş memnuniyetini arttırmak (7 gösterge)
 Hedef 2.2           Kalite kapsamında her yıl değerlendirme toplantıları ve iyileştirme faaliyetleri gerçekleştirmek (3 gösterge)
 Hedef 2.3           Her yıl en az 2 Stratejik Plan ve Kalite Komisyonu değerlendirme toplantısı yapmak (3 gösterge)
 Amaç 3              Uluslararası İş Birlikleri Gerçekleştirmek
 Hedef 3.1           Bölümlerin eğitim, araştırma ve etkinlik gibi faaliyetlerinde uluslararası iş birliğini ve ilişki ağını güçlendirmek (6 gösterge)
 Hedef 3.2           Öğrencilerin ve akademik personelin uluslararası hareketliliğini arttırmak (2 gösterge)
 Hedef 3.3           Gelen öğrenci ve personel sayısını arttırmak (2 gösterge)
 Amaç 4              Araştırma Kapasitesini Arttırmak
 Hedef 4.1           Akademik personelin ulusal ve uluslararası alanda seçkin dergilerdeki yayın sayısını arttırmak (7 gösterge)
 Hedef 4.2           Akademik personeller tarafından yapılan patent ve faydalı model başvuru sayılarını arttırmak (5 gösterge)
 Hedef 4.3           Bilimsel araştırma projeleri başvuru sayılarını arttırmak (7 gösterge)
                     Kütüphanedeki eserleri gelişen teknoloji ve değişen gündeme uygun olarak güncellemek, veri tabanı ve yayın çeşitliliğini arttırmak (6
 Hedef 4.4
                     gösterge)
 Hedef 4.5           Disiplinlerarası çalışmaları arttırmak (4 gösterge)
 Hedef 4.6           Lisansüstü eğitim gören öğrencileri bilimsel yayın ve araştırma faaliyetleri konusunda desteklemek (6 gösterge)
 Hedef 4.7           Öğrenci danışmanlığı ile yapılan araştırma projesi sayısını arttırmak (2 gösterge)
 Amaç 5              Paydaşlarla İlişkileri Güçlendirmek
 Hedef 5.1           Mezun izleme, mezunların istihdam süreçlerini ve lisansüstü eğitim süreçlerini takip etmek (6 gösterge)
                     Dış paydaşlarla ilişkileri güçlendirmek ve izlemek için periyodik olarak işbirliği görüşmeleri gerçekleştirmek Bölgedeki kurumlarla
 Hedef 5.2
                     işbirliğini geliştirmek, süreçlerde görüşlerini almak ve öğrencilerin yararlanabileceği işbirlikleri oluşturmak (4 gösterge)
 Hedef 5.3           Öğrenci kulüplerinin faaliyetlerini arttırmak ve öğrenci katılımını desteklemek (3 gösterge)
 Hedef 5.4           Toplum gereksinimlerine yönelik olarak konferans, seminer vb faaliyetler düzenlemek (7 gösterge)
 Hedef 5.5           Kamu ve Sivil Toplum Kuruluşları ile iş birliğini artırmak (3 gösterge)
 Hedef 5.6           Çevresel, ekonomik ve sosyal boyutlarıyla sürdürülebilir kalkınma amaçlarına yönelik faaliyetleri ve işbirliklerini artırmak (3 gösterge)
                     Sürdürülebilirlik çalışmalarının eğitim-öğretim, araştırma-geliştirme ve toplumsal katkı süreçlerine entegrasyonunu geliştirmek (3
 Hedef 5.7
                     gösterge)

Toplam 5 stratejik amaç, 27 hedef ve 114 performans göstergesi.

ERASMUS
Başvuru Ön Koşulları
Not Ortalaması en az 2.20 olması
İngilizce sınavından 100 üzerinden en az 50 alması gerekir.

Değerlendirme:
• Akademik başarı düzeyi (Genel Not Ortalaması) : %50
• İngilizce sınavı dil puanı : %50
• Engelli öğrencilere +10 puan (Belgelendirilmelidir)
• Şehit ve Gazi çocuklarına +15 puan (Belgelendirilmelidir)
• Daha önce yararlanma (hibeli veya hibesiz) : -10 puan
• Vatandaşı olunan ülkede hareketliliğe katılma: -10 puan
MEZUN SAYIMIZ



16.783
BURS VE İNDİRİMLER
ÖSYM Bursu İndirimi:
Burslu ve %50 İndirimli olarak ÖSYM Kılavuzunda yer alan indirimlerdir.
Tercih İndirimleri:
Tüm bölümlerde geçerli "Sıra Dışı Tercih Bursu" imkanı.
Gaziler, şehit ve gazi yakınları İndirimi:
%10 oranında indirim yapılmaktadır.
Kardeş indirimi:
Her bir kardeşe %5 oranında indirim sağlanır.
Protokol İndirimi:
Yapılan protokoller ışığında lise, kurs merkezi vb. mezunlarına ek indirimler tanımlanabilir.
Eğitimde Katkı ve Başarı Bursu:
Eğitim-Öğretim sırasında her fakültede birer kişi olmak üzere toplam 10 kişiyle sınırlı not
ortalaması 3,50 üzerinde olan öğrencilerimize %100’e kadar varan indirimler verilmektedir.
Öğrencilere Destek İndirimi:
Lisans, Lisansüstü, Ön Lisans ve hazırlık düzeyinde öğrenim gören öğrencilere kapsam ve
esasları belirlenen miktar ve oranlarda indirim yapılabilir.

                                             PREFACE
        Dear Colleagues,
        Welcome to the Fourth International Congress on Women in a Global World: Struggle for
Equality. This significant event, scheduled to take place in İstanbul on May 23-25, 2024, is a
collaborative effort by İstanbul Topkapı University Women and Family Studies Application and
Research Center and numerous esteemed international institutions.
        The primary aim of this congress is to foster an inclusive, multidisciplinary, and global
discussion on women's issues, gender inequality, and the varying ways in which these matters influence
and are influenced by the structures within our societies. This congress provides a platform for
insightful minds from various disciplines to share their research and perspectives on these pressing
issues.
         Contained within this book are abstracts that offer a glimpse into the breadth and depth of the
research that will be shared at the congress. Each abstract has been meticulously crafted, revealing a
snapshot of the larger study it represents. This compilation of abstracts serves as a testament to the
diversity of thought, methodologies, and approaches employed by researchers in this critical field.
         These abstracts offer insights into a wide array of topics related to women's issues. From
education and employment to political representation and social roles, the abstracts underscore the
multifaceted nature of gender and gender discrimination. They highlight how these inequalities are
particularly exacerbated during times of crisis, emphasizing the urgent need for research and action in
these areas.
         The range of topics covered in the abstracts is extensive. It includes women's roles in different
fields and contexts, women's health, feminist activism, women's rights, the representation and rights
of sexual minorities, and more. The abstracts consist of empirical, theoretical, or conceptual research
and policy studies, revealing the universality of women and gender studies.
         The congress, and by extension this book of abstracts, aims to inspire action, enhance
understanding, and contribute significantly to the global struggle for gender equality. The overarching
goal is to pave the way for women worldwide to realize their full potential and contribute meaningfully
to the well-being of societies.
        In closing, we would like to express our deepest gratitude to all contributors. Each abstract in
this book is a fine product of your dedication, hard work, and commitment to advancing knowledge in
your respective fields. Your contributions not only broaden our understanding of the issues at hand but
also play a crucial role in shaping the discourse at the Fourth International Congress on Women in a
Global World: Struggle for Equality. The impact of your work extends beyond the confines of this
congress, influencing policies, informing practices, and inspiring future research. Thank you for your
unwavering commitment to the global cause of equality for all.
                           Assoc. Prof. Dr. Zeynep Banu DALAMAN
                                         Congress Chair

     ABSTRACTS




                         18
                       International Congress on Women in A Global World IV
                          Uluslararası Küresel Dünyada Kadın Kongresi IV

  DEVELOPMENT OF THE WOMEN'S LEADERSHIP PERCEPTION
                       SCALE
                                                                                                    Ahmet KILIÇ*

Abstract
In recent years, different types of leadership have been the subject of research. Accordingly, leadership
definitions also change. When definitions related to leadership are researched, it is seen that many
definitions have been made and there is no clear consensus. However, in general leadership definitions;
It can be said that key concepts such as influence, guide, trust and goal come to the fore. Leadership
can be defined in the broadest sense as influencing followers and directing them to the desired goal.
The subject of leadership attracts the attention of researchers in different scientific fields. For this
reason, it is often possible to see interdisciplinary studies on leadership. In addition, the majority of
male researchers among scientists interested in women's studies also draws attention. According to
research, the factors that hinder the careers of female employees include management-psychology
relationship, working conditions, superior-subordinate relationship, problems in the work environment,
etc. It is seen that there are a limited number of scale studies on female leadership. The aim of the
research conducted in this direction is; To develop a reliable and valid measurement tool to measure
secondary school teachers' perception of female leadership. First of all, a domestic and international
literature review was conducted on the subject of women's leadership, and 12 items were developed
within the framework of the information obtained after interviews with secondary school teachers and
expert academics in the field. The remaining items will be subjected to a preliminary application, the
clarity of the items will be tested, and items that are deemed inappropriate will be removed again. In
the next process, the sample numbers selected randomly among secondary school teachers working in
Gaziantep Nizip district center (2023-2024), which constitute the research population, will be
determined. After the number of items consisting of five-point Likert-type positive expressions is
finalized, validity and reliability tests will be carried out by using the SPSS statistical program in the
factor analysis of the responses to the scale items. It is thought that the research will fill an important
literature gap on women's leadership with this unique aspect.
Keywords: Woman, leadership, female leadership, scale.

  NAVIGATING BARRIERS WITH COMPASSION IN A VIRTUAL
LABORATORY EXPERIENCE TO DELIVER HUMANIZED CARE TO
       IMMIGRANT WOMEN: INITIAL FRAMEWORK
    1. Alessia BISIO*, 2. Enrique YEGUAS-BOLIVAR†, 3. Pilar APARICIO-MARTINEZ‡, 4. Juri
                               TABORRI§, 5. Aurora RUIZ MEZCUA**

Abstract
The human environment's profound impact on individual health encompasses all aspects of life,
including guaranteeing life itself and moral, ethical, and physical integrity, forming the basis of
humanised care. It is considered that the interventions developed, and comprehensive perinatal care
have been conditioned by an evident reduction in the rate of individualised assistance, which can be
limited by barriers such as lack of resources, language, and cultural differences. Among the negative
emotions produced by a lack of care resources, deficient care practices, or exclusion in maternal and
paternal decision-making, postpartum depression stands out since it is one of the most prevalent
psycho-emotional disorders. Within maternal well-being, the convergence of socio-emotional and
environmental factors defines the quality of care. Thus, this line of inquiry seeks to explore how the
dehumanisation of maternal care during childbirth influences the development of postpartum
depression, recognising the intricate interplay between healthcare practices and mental health
outcomes. To avoid the dehumanisation and issues related to care, it is highly relevant to provide
training regarding maternal care, barriers and compassion. Moreover, adequate and humanised
maternal care is lacking, especially for immigrant pregnant women. According to the World Health
Organization (WHO), the rate of dehumanization is 49.4% and postpartum depression is 20%. Such
levels could be even higher in immigrant women due to the language and cultural differences. Despite
this, the training of healthcare professionals, from midwives to doctors, can bridge this gap between
the WHO's recommendations and the current rates of inadequate care. Implementing a virtual reality
(VR) laboratory tailored to educate healthcare providers could effectively engage with immigrant
women by elucidating the myriad barriers both parties encounter and guiding them through
standardised tasks to surmount these obstacles. This immersive platform could provide professionals
with insights into maternal care from a holistic view, including cultural and communicative
perspectives, enabling personalized and culturally humanized care. The VR laboratory raises
awareness and compassion for minority issues and equips professionals with cultural competencies to
overcome barriers. Ultimately, it aims to enhance pregnancy outcomes and promote holistic well-being
while advocating for inclusivity and equity in healthcare delivery through humanized care.
Keywords: Maternal care, humanization, inclusion, immigration, virtual reality.

         “TAKING DOWN THE WALLS”: ENHANCING WOMEN’S
                  JUDICIARY REPRESENTATION
                            1. Anmol SHARMA*, 2. Sunita Singh KHATANA†

Abstract
For many years, it has been acknowledged that men predominate in the Indian judiciary. As the
backbone of any democracy, the judiciary represents the values of justice, equity, and equality before
the law. But these core principles are seriously threatened by the striking underrepresentation of women
in the court. This paper examines the lack of diversity in the Indian judiciary and how it underrepresents
women. It indicates standardised practices like discrimination and gender stereotypes. Using a feminist
lens, recruitment materials on diversity and biases should be gender-sensitive, and women in law
should have access to support systems. The paper examines the social implications and contributing
factors to the underrepresentation of women in the Indian judiciary. The first half of this research paper
talks about the history of women in Indian judiciary, what are the contributing factors to the
underrepresentation of women in Indian judiciary, and how this affects society. What are the potential
outcomes of having a low number of female judges? In the second, it was discussed what steps are
being taken to encourage women and how they can contribute to the advancement of gender justice. It
goes on to discuss a comparative research project with other nations. Finally, the paper explains the
best practices for promoting gender equality in the judiciary. It concludes, in the end, that the
importance of advancing the representation of women in the judiciary is a crucial component of broader
efforts to achieve gender equality and strengthen the rule of law. These reforms aim to create a more
inclusive judiciary that reflects the diverse population of India and ensures equitable access to justice.
Keywords: Discrimination, diversity, gender equality, justice, underrepresentation.

  DOES THE CLIMATE CRISIS AFFECT GENDER INEQUALITY?: A
                BIBLIOMETRIC ANALYSIS
                                                                                                 Aslı ÖZPOLAT*

Abstract
Climate change is one of the most important issues on a global and regional scale. As stated in climate
change report (IPPC, 2023), Global temperature has increased by 1.1 degrees in the last decade (2011-
2020) compared to the period 1850-1900. It is stated that this temperature increase will affect
approximately 3.3 million to 3.6 million people and that these people are quite vulnerable to climate
change. As a result of this temperature increase, many problems such as food shortage, clean water
availability, infectious diseases, biosystem degradation, forest fires, gender inequality and migration
occur. At COP 28, studies to be carried out in this field were included and the measures to be taken by
countries were discussed. In particular, the relationship between gender inequality and climate change
attracts attention in terms of preventing poverty. For this purpose, the relationship between climate
change and gender inequality, and the effects of climate change especially on women, will be examined
within the framework of a bibliometric analysis between 1986-2024 in the web science database. The
analysis was examined under two clusters called climate change and gender inequality. The analysis
includes keyword analysis, author analysis, country analysis, citation analysis of bibliometric authors,
citation analysis of bibliometric articles and co- citation analysis of bibliometric references. In this
context, there are a total of 6,832 studies in the web of science database. The most studies were
conducted in the field of environmental sciences (2,118), and the second study was in the field of
meteorology atmospheric sciences (1,008). Among these studies, 990 studies were conducted in the
field of economics. Looking at the distribution of studies by years, the most studies were conducted in
2023 (1,476). When we look at the types of publications, it is seen that the most articles (4,932) were
made, and the second place was master's theses (750). Therefore, the paper provides an overview of
how the literature on gender inequality and climate change has developed by examining these studies
in the literature, as well as revealing the trends and gaps in the literature.
Keywords: Gender justice, climate change, women.

ozpolat@gantep.edu.tr

                       International Congress on Women in A Global World IV
                          Uluslararası Küresel Dünyada Kadın Kongresi IV

   GENDERED DISINFORMATION TOWARDS WOMEN'S HEALTH:
           ANTI-HPV VACCINE ON SOCIAL MEDIA
                                                                                   Aslıhan ARDIÇ ÇOBANER*

Abstract
When we accept technology as a part of the socialization process, we cannot think of digital tools as
independent from gender relations. It can be said that technology is gendered in many areas, from its
design to its accessibility and its incorporation into everyday life practices, that is, its use. In addition,
digital technologies and new media redefine gender relations and are a new space for women to express
themselves. Donna Haraway (2006), one of those who emphasize the power of digital technologies,
argues that with the emergence of digital technologies, women have had the chance to act outside their
biological sex and the gender roles assigned to them. However, subsequent studies have shown that
these platforms alone are not enough to transform gender relations and produce new inequalities. In
fact, examples of women's silencing as a patriarchal practice in offline spaces are increasingly being
seen in online spaces as well. One of the strategies used to silence women is “gendered disinformation”.
In the literature, sexist disinformation or sexualized disinformation is defined as online disinformation
based on the coordinated dissemination of false or misleading information against women using
gendered and racialized narratives. As a form of "digital misogyny", it aims to silence women who
openly voice their opinions on social media and discourage them from participating in the public
sphere. This study aims to examine the comments on Instagram influencer Ecem Taşer's (@ecemtaser)
post in which she and her husband announced that both she and her husband had received the HPV
vaccine as a threat to women's health and an example of sexist disinformation. User comments on
Ecem Taşer's post on HPV vaccination and the comments on these comments were analyzed through
thematic analysis. Based on a case study, this study aims to understand the effects of sexist
disinformation on women's lives.
Keywords: Gendered disinformation, digital misogyny, HPV vaccines, social media, Instagram.

                                                                                            Turkish Presentation




* Prof. Dr., Mersin University, Journalism, Türkiye, ORCID: 0000-0002-0029-4181, email: acobaner@mersin.edu.tr




                                                         23
                       International Congress on Women in A Global World IV
                          Uluslararası Küresel Dünyada Kadın Kongresi IV

      RESISTANCE AND EMPOWERED BLACK FEMALE BODY IN
        GRACE NICHOLS’ THE FAT BLACK WOMAN’S POEMS
                                                                                              Atakan SALCAN*

Abstract
The literature from regions like the Caribbean, heavily colonised by Europe, inevitably reflects the
colonial experiences endured. Living under such pressures shapes both the lives and literature of
individuals. Today, even subtle details bear the imprint of colonial oppressions, particularly in how
Europe marginalises 'White' content. For instance, Grace Nichols' 1984 poetry collection, The Fat
Black Woman's Poems, explores themes of race, identity, and gender with impactful language.
Celebrating the strength and beauty of black women, Nichols challenges stereotypes and societal
norms, offering an empowering perspective. The standout poem addresses body image and self-
acceptance, showcasing Nichols' vibrant language and Caribbean roots. Postcolonial individuals face
triple marginalization due to intersectional oppression. Some strive to resist these pressures by
illustrating examples of resistance in their works. The Fat Black Woman's Poems stands as a significant
work addressing themes of race, feminism, and cultural identity. Grace Nichols' distinct voice and
thematic depth make it a notable contribution to poetry. Through her poems, Nichols confronts
marginalizations based on gender, race, and body, striving to resist them. She connects past and future
in "The Fat Black Woman Remembers," explores the relationship between fashion and identity in "The
Fat Black Woman Goes Shopping," and critiques Eurocentric beauty standards in "Looking at Miss
World." In this direction, this paper aims to explore how the lyrical I in three poems from The Fat Black
Woman's Poems experiences marginalization based on race, gender, and beauty standards in the black
female body framework, employing an intersectional lens. The main argument is to illustrate how
Grace Nichols portrays the marginalizations of being black, female, and fat in these poems, while also
demonstrating the poet's efforts to resist them.
Keywords: The Fat Black Woman’s Poems, intersectionality, race, gender, beauty standards,
marginalisation.

email: salcan336@gmail.com

    CONCEPT OF HONOR TOWARDS WOMEN: TÜRKİYE SAMPLE
                        STUDY
                            1. Ayhan BABAROĞLU*, 2. Derya Yüksel KOÇAK†

Abstract
In this cross-sectional and descriptive study, it was aimed to determine the understanding of honour
regarding women and affecting factors. The sample included 1053 adults over the age of 17, from 7
geographical regions in Turkey. The data were collected with Demographic Information Form and
Honor Concept Perception Toward Women Scale (KINATO). Descriptive statistics, Mann Whitney-U
test, Kruskall Wallis test and Spearman correlation were used in the analysis through SPSS Windows
22.0 (Statistical Package of Social Sicences, 2010) program. The participants were 79% female, 21%
male, mostly urban, middle-income, highly educated, and almost half of them were between the ages
of 18-25. In the study, the average total score of the participants from KINATO was calculated as
103,197. In the study, it was determined that the understanding of honor towards women differs
statistically significantly according to age, gender, education level, type of marriage, family type,
income level, city and region of residence, and that the variables of marital status and employment
status do not make a statistically significant difference. It was determined that the participants in the
18-25 age were more distant from the traditional understanding of honour than the older participants,
that the female participants had a more positive understanding of honour regarding women, and that
the participants with master's/doctorate level education and the participants whose spouses were
primary school graduates had a negative understanding of honour. While the honour perception of the
participants who had arranged marriages was more negative than the participants who were single and
had love marriages, the honour perception of women improved as the income level increased.
Participants with extended family have a negative understanding of honour compared to participants
with nuclear family and single-parent family, and participants living in metropolitan areas have a
positive understanding of honour. It can be said that the participants' understanding of honour regarding
women is egalitarian and positive, but the traditional perspective continues to exist. It is important to
increase the level of education and improve the income level of women by being present in the public
sphere in order to change the understanding of honour towards an egalitarian.
Keywords: Women, honour, gender, culture, Türkiye.

       WOMEN IN THE SECOND WORLD WAR: DISCOURSES AND
         POLICIES TOWARDS WOMEN IN THE CONTEXT OF
             REPRODUCTION AND SOCIAL CONSENT
                                     Ayla Ezgi AKYOL GIAGTZOGLOU*

Abstract
Wars were periods when, the state’s concern for legitimacy, its need for the social consent relatively
increased. In such a conjuncture, women as a social subject find more space on the agenda of political
elites. One of the main reasons for this is that the political support of the male population at the front
for the mobilization policy is closely tied to ensuring the livelihood of their families and guaranteeing
the role of their wives in the household. Another reason is that women at the home front become more
dependent on wages, they are also more affected by the economic consequences of the war such as the
black market, inflation, price speculation and lack of access to necessities; this creates a social
contradiction that needs to be resolved by political elites. A third reason is that the control and discipline
of female fertility has become one of the main issues of politics due to the social role of women in the
reproduction of “human resources”. Thus, from the reproduction of social consent during wartime to
the management of poverty, from the legitimization of war to the continuity of production and social
reproduction, women become one of the main agendas of political elites. During the Second World
War, although the Republic of Turkey did not officially participate in the war, a policy of mobilization
was adopted, a statist war economy based on excessive taxation was pursued and access to necessities
became difficult due to the high rate of inflation, price speculation and black market. This article
scrutinizes the involvement of women in the political agenda during the Second World War. Drawing
upon the reports of the Parliament and state archives and newspapers and magazines of the era, it
investigates the discourse crafted by political actors to reinforce the war’s legitimacy vis-à-vis women.
It also examines the policies pursued by political elites to address the increasing poverty among women
during the war, along with the contradictions experienced by women in production and reproduction
processes. Furthermore, the article addresses the formulation of social policies concerning reproduction
of women’s domestic societal roles, family and birth control.
Keywords: Second World War, women, war economy, reproduction, legitimacy, social consent.

                                                                                               Turkish Presentation

email: ezgi.akyol@istanbul.edu.tr

    WOMEN-FRIENDLY CITIES: GENDER EQUALITY AND URBAN
                           LIFE
                      1. Aysun PERİM KETENCİLER*, 2. Meryem METİNOĞLU†

Abstract
The aim of the research is to increase awareness of the concept of 'women-friendly cities' in order to
create safer, more inclusive, and supportive urban environments for women. In line with this aim,
emphasis is placed on highlighting women's experiences, needs, and demands in urban areas to
encourage the development of women-friendly policies by cities. This way, the goal is to enhance the
quality of life for women in cities and promote gender equality. The city is a living space where social,
political, administrative, and economic areas exist for all citizens. It is estimated that more than 50%
of the world's population lives in cities. Urbanization is particularly significant in terms of various
factors such as job opportunities, education, age, gender, and social status. The way women and men
use urban areas, the needs that must be met in urban spaces, and the level and frequency of benefiting
from the provided services differ. Ignoring the differences based on gender also leads to exclusion from
urban spaces and services provided. To eliminate the secondary position of women in urban life, the
"Woman-Friendly Cities Project" was initiated in Europe under the auspices of the United Nations
(UN). The main goal of the project is to create woman-friendly cities at both local and national levels
through activities conducted with women civil society organizations, grassroots organizations, and
government institutions. A woman-friendly city is an urban management approach that ensures
women's rights are protected, allows their participation in local decision-making processes, and bases
the management and regulation of the city on gender equality. For a city to be woman-friendly, it is
crucial to identify the problems and needs of women and take the necessary measures. These measures
have been determined as providing spatial and security services, enabling women to be decision-
makers at the local level, establishing equality units, and preparing strategic plans sensitive to gender
equality. A woman feeling safe and at peace in a city also enhances the welfare of the family and
community. Therefore, increasing the number of woman-friendly cities is of great importance both in
our country and other countries.
Keywords: Woman, woman-friendly city, urbanization.

 THE ROLE OF WOMEN IN THE DEMOCRATIZATION PERIOD:
GENDER OF POLITICAL REPRESENTATION AND PARTICIPATION
                                           Aysun YARALI-AKKAYA*

Abstract
Democracies, which have been defined as the will of the people and the government of the people since
ancient times, equal representation and participation of all segments of society is indispensable. The
increase in the number of women representatives in parliaments in recent years points to the
empowerment of women in areas such as gender equality and development (Walby, 2000:20). Even if
democracies are historically or ontologically more masculine (Phillips, 2012:18), it is also recognized
that they can be strengthened with women. Therefore, it is important to increase women's participation
and representation in politics. In this study, the contribution of gender equality to the democratization
of politics will be explained with data on representation and participation. A comparison will be made
between participation, representation and electoral systems and factors such as economic development
and welfare level in the realization of the principles of equality, freedom, rule of law and popular rule
(Sartori, 1993:376, Dahl, 1956:4, Schumpeter, 1976:142), which are the defining elements of
democracy. An answer will be sought to the question of whether increasing participation alone is
sufficient to strengthen democracies and how issues such as diversity, diversity, pluralism and gender
have become indicators of strengthening democracies in recent years. How democracies are
strengthened with women and what the indicators related to women in Turkey mean. Therefore, this
study will first draw a theoretical framework with a literature review, explain the basic elements of
democracies, and then look at the relationship between women and democracy with the question of
whether democracy has a gender. Then, the contribution of increasing women's participation and
representation to democracies will be analyzed with data from the world and Turkey. The collected
data will be analyzed using the critical method. As a result, it will be concluded that democracy is not
only synonymous with equal representation of women in political institutions and the obstacles to
women's participation in politics will be examined. In this case, in addition to increasing women's
political participation or increasing their representation rates, democracies need to ensure gender
equality in all areas. Not only women, but also the demands of other marginalized groups should be
taken into consideration. Women are more sensitive than men not only for their own struggle for rights
but also for disadvantaged groups and can fight for them. It would be sexist to explain women's
heightened awareness in terms of biology or gender, but it would not be wrong to say that women's
historical background as a gender increases this sensitivity.
Keywords: Women, participation, democracy, representation, gender.

aysunyarali@mersin.edu.tr

 THE INVISIBLE LABOUR OF WOMEN IN FOSTER CARE WHICH
BECAME POPULAR AFTER THE FEBRUARY 6 KAHRAMANMARAŞ
                     EARTHQUAKE
                                          Ayşe Betül TANRIVERDİ*

Abstract
After the 6 February Kahramanmaraş earthquake, interest and applications for foster care increased.
With this increase in interest, the public acted emotionally and the compassion vein came to the fore.
After the earthquake, foster family was misunderstood by the public and therefore there was a great
increase in applications. It remains a mystery as to why many people who applied to become foster
parents for earthquake victims applied and to what extent they have knowledge about the nature of
foster care. In foster care, the woman becomes the object who takes care of the child. The child is
commodified by the media and after all the processes are completed, the child is mostly taken care of
by the woman and the responsibility is assumed by the woman. The woman takes care of the physical
and emotional care of the child; she also makes the child the centre of her life with the anxiety caused
by the control mechanism. Such a responsibility negatively affects the woman's private life; her career
plan is only the future of the child. Although raising children is a sacred endeavour, the fact that it is
socially seen as a woman's duty brings along a number of problems. In foster care, women come to the
forefront; this situation brings emotional labour exploitation for women. In this study, it is tried to
understand why many families who want to become a foster family for earthquake victims after the
earthquake on 6 February applied, what they aim for, and the invisible labour of women in foster care
are tried to be understood from internet newspaper news and comments made on social media accounts.
The sample of the research is the news with the keyword "foster family" published in online
newspapers between February-December 2023 and the posts on the official page of the Ministry of
Family and Social Services. The sample of the study is limited to the year 2023 because the subject of
the study is to investigate the increasing popularity after the 6 February Kahramanmaraş earthquake.
In the study, content analysis technique is used by using qualitative research method. In content
analysis through coding, important parts are described and interpreted.
Keywords: Foster care, children, women's labour, media.

abetultanriverdi@cumhuriyet.edu.tr

HEALTH POLICIES THAT EMPOWER WOMEN DURING DISASTER
                                          Ayşe DELİKTAŞ DEMİRCİ*

Abstract
The hazards of natural disasters and infectious diseases are expanding rapidly in the contemporary era,
posing a growing threat to individuals around the world. Although these catastrophes have an impact
on everyone, women, who are often part of vulnerable populations, are disproportionately affected
during these times. The circumstances of disasters intensify gender roles, resulting in increased
vulnerability to sexual and nonsexual violence for women. Moreover, women often experience
economic hardships during disasters and may face difficulties accessing healthcare services.
Specifically, women with special health needs, such as pregnancy, childbirth, and childcare, are at a
higher risk during disasters. To address the unique needs and challenges of women during disasters,
health policies that empower them are essential. The objective of this study is to investigate the
characteristics of health policies that enable women to overcome the challenges they face during
disaster periods. The literature review suggests that effective strategies include gender-sensitive health
services, inclusive disaster planning, capacity-building and training programs, active participation of
women in decision-making processes, promotion of community resilience, and fostering leadership
roles. Gender-sensitive health services involve providing access to reproductive health services,
maternal care, mental health support, and addressing gender-based violence issues. Inclusive disaster
planning aims to address women’s specific needs and challenges in policies, disaster planning, and
response processes. Empowering women during disasters is crucial to ensuring their safety and well-
being. To achieve this, it is essential to incorporate gender analysis into risk assessments and design
interventions considering women's vulnerabilities and strengths. Additionally, training can be provided
on gender-sensitive care, trauma-informed approaches, and recognizing and responding to gender-
based violence. Empowering women involves actively involving them in decision-making processes
and promoting leadership roles, which allows for collaboration and ensures that their voices are heard.
Policies should also include gender-based data collection and analysis to understand the impact of
disasters on women's health outcomes, enabling the evaluation of intervention effectiveness, the
development of evidence-based policies, and continuous improvements. In conclusion, health policies
aimed at empowering women during disasters should be comprehensive and gender-sensitive.
Keywords: Disasters, women, global age, sex.

                                                                                             English Presentation




* Asst. Prof., Akdeniz University, Obstetric and Gynecological Nursing, Türkiye, ORCID: 0000-0003-0872-2043, e-mail:

aysedeliktas@akdeniz.edu.tr


                                                          30
                      International Congress on Women in A Global World IV
                         Uluslararası Küresel Dünyada Kadın Kongresi IV

     STRATEGIES TO STRENGTHEN WOMEN'S VULNERABILITY
                      DURING DISASTER
                                          Ayşe Handan DÖKMECİ*

Abstract
Regardless of the natural or human-made disaster experienced, the level of impact and effects on
individuals may vary. It is known that individuals, whom we define as vulnerable and fragile groups
in society, are more likely to be affected by disasters due to different impacts. The resources and powers
of individuals affected by natural disasters are not equal. In general, women, children, the elderly and
disabled people are more vulnerable than adult men, and this vulnerability causes their ability to adapt
to the environment and survive as a result of a disaster to be lower. Vulnerable and fragile individuals
are less likely to take action to protect themselves before, during or after disasters. They are also known
to be at greater risk for poor physical and psychological health outcomes after a disaster. The
individuals most affected by disasters are generally those in these groups. Considering the gender factor
for these groups, it is seen that women are more affected by disasters than men. Women are among the
most affected groups due to their physical characteristics, mental state, education level and health. The
disaster risk reduction community has long recognized that risk is systemic, and gender inequality is a
big part of that. While some strides have been made in women's leadership and empowerment in
advancing resilience at the municipal level in terms of establishing a risk reduction agenda, there is
still work to be done. While climate change, COVID-19, the Ukraine-Russia and Israel-Palestine crises
are at the forefront of our minds, it is important to remember that gender inequality impedes global
progress everywhere, at all times, and can pose an even greater threat if left unaddressed. As a result,
aside from combating natural disasters, to ensure and continue sustainable development in the world,
no individual should be included in plans and programs without being left behind. Within the scope of
these programs, it is extremely important to strengthen our female individuals, who are exposed to
gender discrimination and experience its negative effects due to their vulnerable and fragile position in
society. In this study, strategies to empower women against disasters will be discussed and solution
suggestions will be sought.
Keywords: Women, disaster, vulnerability, resilience.

    VIOLENCE AGAINST WOMEN AND THE EFFECTS OF VIOLENCE
         ON WOMEN'S MENTAL HEALTH ON THE BASIS OF
                      CRIMINOLOGY*
                                1. Azize Serap TUNÇER†, 2. Elif Cemre İZGİ‡

Abstract
The concept of violence is a social problem that is the subject of various scientific research. This social
problem also has a direct relationship with the phenomenon of crime. Crime and violence are two
issues that basically trigger and negatively affect each other. Criminology, also known as crime science,
is closely related to the concept of violence. In this study, the concept of violence is examined on the
basis of criminology and the concept of violence is considered as a crime phenomenon. Since violence
and crime occur at the focus of the individual, violence significantly affects both the physical and
mental health of the individual who is exposed to it. After examining violence on the basis of
criminology, the study focused on the relationship between violence and the mental health of the
individual. The individual focus of the study was women, who are among sensitive/fragile groups who
witness and are exposed to almost all types of violence in many social and private areas. Women and
girls can witness and be exposed to violence at a very early age. Women can be exposed to violence,
which is discussed on the basis of crime in this study, at home, on the street, at work and in many areas.
This study was prepared with the focus of the review conducted in February-March 2024. The study
was prepared by scanning the national and international literature on the topics of violence against
women, criminology, mental health and violence, which form the basis of the subject discussed. There
are mechanisms through which women who are subjected to violence can complain about this criminal
act, but their function is not sufficient. As a result of the violence they are exposed to, women have
difficulty accessing the necessary defense mechanisms and cannot benefit from the protection
mechanisms to the extent necessary. The mental health of women who are both exposed to violence
and face many problems after the violence is greatly affected by the negative process they experience.
It is important to increase and diversify studies on the mental health of women, especially after the
violence they are exposed to. Violence, one of the most important criminal acts, needs to be eliminated
by strengthening existing policies and producing new applicable policies. It is inevitable that
ineffective penalties and inadequate protection mechanisms will increase not only violence but also
many criminal acts in society.
Keywords: Criminology, mental health, violence, violence against women.

BEING A EZIDI WOMAN IN TURKEY: A SOCIAL PSYCHOLOGICAL
                     EVALUATION
                            1. B. Dilara ŞEKER*, 2. Gülfidan GÜNDOĞMUŞ†

Abstract
In today's societies, groups with different cultural, ethnic and religious backgrounds live together.
Culture is the set of shared assumptions, beliefs, myths, values, and rituals that shape how a group of
people think, feel and act in the world around them. One of the ethnic and religious groups living in
Turkey is the Yazidi group, which is relatively small in number. Yazidi is a religious group originally
belonging to the Kurdish ethnicity, and they mostly live in Batman, Diyarbakır, Gaziantep, Şanlıurfa,
and Mardin. The individual's identity is formed by defining himself/herself and positioning
himself/herself concerning others. The groups an individual is a member of are an essential source of
reference in the individual's identity. Social identity emphasizes differences and provides individuals
with identification models to define themselves. This identification is an essential factor that affects
the individual's definition of his identity and the use of social categories. In this process, the individual
feels cognitively, emotionally, and behaviorally stable. However, social identity developed in
connection with a disadvantaged/minority group may not always benefit the individual. In this case, it
is known that the discrimination perceived by the individual increases. This study aims to reveal how
Yazidi women living in Şalıurfa define themselves and their daily life experiences. The experiences of
Yazidi women will be discussed in the context of social psychological concepts such as social identity,
intersectionality, and discrimination. The sample of the study consists of seven resident Yazidi women.
Participants were included in the study using the snowball sampling method, one of the purposeful
sampling methods. Research data was collected using a semi-structured interview form consisting of
open-ended questions developed by the researchers. The data obtained in the study were evaluated with
phenomenological analysis. It is aimed to reach the essence of the participants' experiences on the
subject. In the study, a systematic process consisting of the stages of bracketing, phenomenological
reduction, imaginative diversification, and synthesis of meanings and essences was followed in the
analysis of the data. As a result of the data analysis, the interrelated components that constitute the
structure and essence of the phenomenon of being a Yazidi woman were revealed. In the study, it was
observed that women primarily defined themselves as Yazidi and Kurdish. Participants stated that they
did not differ from the larger community in terms of daily life practices but differed in traditions.
Participants also stated that they differed in terms of religious practices. It was observed that the
participants experienced discrimination because they were identified as Yazidis and generally preferred
to interact with members of their group. The study is critical because it sheds light on the lives of Yazidi
women, a small disadvantaged group living in Turkey.
Keywords: Yazidi women, social identity, intersectionality, discrimination.

  WORKING WOMEN LIVING IN THE NEW NORMAL: CHANGE IN
                   GENDER ROLES
                                               B. Dilara ŞEKER*

Abstract
Today's crises, pandemics, and disasters cause uncertainty and affect people's daily lives, gender roles,
and access to resources. Especially in epidemics such as the COVID-19 pandemic, the existential threat
always present in human life becomes evident. This situation can be explained socially and
psychologically by terror management theory. In this process, the individual may experience
uncertainty, and the order that the individual is familiar with is shaken. According to the theory, as the
existential threat (death threat) becomes evident, many psychological, social, cultural and economic
changes may occur in the individual's life. The individual can manage his thoughts about this existential
threat using two defence mechanisms. These are proximal defence mechanisms, activated against
conscious awareness of concerns about existential threat (death), and distal defence mechanisms,
activated when the person does not have a focal concern. Therefore, the individual adopts cultural
worldviews to feel safe. One of these is traditional gender roles. Gender roles are based on the cultural
interpretation of individuals' innate biological differences. Thus, social expectations are developed
about who has or should have what rights and power for men and women. This structure causes gender-
based inequalities. It is known that the inequalities experienced by women solely due to their gender
increase, especially during crisis periods such as pandemics and natural disasters, when the normal
flow of life changes. The study aims to address the change in gender roles of women working under
existential threat. The study will reveal how the participants evaluate their experiences in their home
and work areas. For this reason, the reactions of working women as existential threats became apparent
were evaluated in the study. Seventeen working women living in Izmir participated in the study. Semi-
structured interviews were conducted with the participants. The data were evaluated in the research
with thematic analysis, which summarizes the basic features and allows flexible and rich descriptions.
As a result of the study, it was revealed that women's workload increased in the home and work
environment during the pandemic period when the existential threat became evident. It was also
observed that the inequalities and responsibilities of the participants increased during this period. It
was found that the participants used proximal and distal defence mechanisms to cope with these
problems. Understanding these mechanisms will benefit the individual in the short term and the society
in the long term. The study also emphasizes the urgency of addressing the increasing gender
inequalities under women's existential threat.
Keywords: Gender roles, existential threat, working women, terror management theory, defence
mechanisms.

dilaraseker@yyu.edu.tr

   STATISTICAL ANALYSIS OF ACCESS TO K-12 EDUCATION FOR
            GIRLS IN TURKEY BY REGIONAL UNITS
                                         Berna ERDOĞAN YOLDAŞ*

Abstract
The concept of equality of opportunity in education refers to inequalities in individuals' access to and
utilisation of educational opportunities. Ensuring equality of opportunity in education is of great
importance for social development as well as individual development. One of the most important
problems in access to education is the lack of equal opportunities. Factors such as gender, socio-
economic, political, cultural differences and geographical factors cause differences in access to
education. In this context, in this study, in order to reveal the effect of regional development on girls'
access to education, 2nd level regional units are considered. This study aims to determine the access
of girls to education compared to boys in Turkey and regional differences in girls' access to education
by considering regional differences and gender variables. In addition, the comparison and analysis of
the rates of girls' access to education at different levels in the historical process are emphasised. The
research was designed using the document analysis technique, which is one of the qualitative research
methods. Through document analysis, a detailed regional analysis of girls' access to education has been
applied. The official statistics of the Ministry of National Education between 2006 and 2023 were used
as the data source for the purpose of the study. The collected data were analysed through document
analysis method and the degree of increase in girls' access to education in Turkey, regional differences
in access to education compared to boys and the consequences of changes in education policies in
Turkey were discussed. With this study, when the total participation of girls and boys in education is
analysed, it is seen that the inequality of opportunity in access to education at the K12 level in terms
of gender has disappeared. At the same time, it has been concluded that there is no regional difference
in girls' access to education at the primary education level, while when the secondary education level
is analysed, it has been concluded that girls' access to education is lower in some 2nd level regions
compared to other regions. In addition, it has been revealed that the difference in the access rates of
male and female students to education in these regions has increased compared to other 2nd level
regions. This study provides significant progress in raising awareness about girls' access to education
in geographies where girls' access to education is low in Level 2 regions and providing guidance to
policy makers.
Keywords: Education, inequality of opportunity, schooling rates.

 THE WOMEN IN WORLD THEATER HISTORY UNDER THE TITLE
                 OF WOMEN AND ART
                                                Burak AKYÜZ*

Abstract
The reflection of many of the problems experienced by women in today's social life has also been
frequently seen in the history of theater art, which is called 'Reflection of Life'. The role and influence
of women in the art of drama, which takes all its material from life itself, has been a subject of
discussion for centuries, and all the difficulties that women face in social life are also reflected in the
art of theater itself. When we look at the history of theater, actors, directors, writers, etc. In all the
elements that serve the theatre, the scarcity of women in number stands out. For many centuries,
playwriting has been carried out effectively by men. Ancient Greek theater (400s BC), which can be
considered as the first examples of written text in the history of world theater, was able to bring the
issue of women to the stage, but could not see its solution in society. It is known that women, who are
generally evaluated only for their fertility, do not have the same rights as men in society. In the play
'Medea', written by an author named Euripides during the ancient Greek period, the position of women
in society was brought into question. According to what we can infer from the lines in the play, it is
seen that the women of the period did not have the chance to express themselves. Medea was betrayed
by her husband Jason and killed her own children in revenge. As far as we understand from the play,
the lines in the play say that a woman is a weak person and it is difficult for her to live without being
dominated by a man. We know that in ancient Greece, female roles were played by male actors. The
ancient Greek actor uses giant masks on his face to create the character on stage. Although masks
representing women are used, female roles are always played by male actors. So why couldn't women
exist on stage for a long time in world theater history (literally until the 1700s)? Female roles were
played by male actors (also during Shakespeare's time). The main reason for this may be that the female
actress on stage is evaluated as having low moral quality. Since this situation partially continues today
- which is the main subject of the paper - it can be said that similar problems also exist in theater art.
Keywords: Actress, woman and stage, woman in theatre.
